    // Strip raw HTML typed by users; alignment and YouTube are handled via extensions.
    // Override the image renderer: if the title is a plain number (e.g. "300" or "300px")
    // treat it as a pixel width rather than an alt-title.  max-width:100% CSS then
    // scales it down further when the container is narrower than the specified width.
    // Enable hard line-breaks so a single newline renders as <br>
    marked.use({ breaks: true });
    marked.use({
      renderer: {
        html: function() { return ''; },
        image: function(token) {
          var src   = token.href  || '';
          var alt   = (token.text  || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
          var title = token.title || '';
          var wm    = /^(\d+)(?:px)?$/.exec(title.trim());
          if (wm) {
            return '<img src="' + src + '" alt="' + alt + '" width="' + wm[1] + '">';
          }
          var ta = title ? ' title="' + title.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '"' : '';
          return '<img src="' + src + '" alt="' + alt + '"' + ta + '>';
        }
      }
    });

    // Helper: extract an 11-char YouTube video ID from a URL or bare ID.
    function extractYouTubeId(input) {
      var m = input.match(/(?:v=|youtu\.be\/|embed\/)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{11})/);
      if (m) return m[1];
      if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(input)) return input;
      return null;
    }

    marked.use({
      extensions: [
        // ::: left/center/right … ::: — alignment blocks
        {
          name: 'alignBlock',
          level: 'block',
          start: function(src) { return src.indexOf('\n:::'); },
          tokenizer: function(src) {
            var m = /^:::\s*(left|center|right)\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n?:::\s*(?:\r?\n|$)/i.exec(src);
            if (m) {
              var token = { type: 'alignBlock', raw: m[0], align: m[1].toLowerCase(), tokens: [] };
              this.lexer.blockTokens(m[2].trim(), token.tokens);
              return token;
            }
          },
          renderer: function(token) {
            return '<div align="' + token.align + '">' + this.parser.parse(token.tokens) + '</div>\n';
          }
        },
        // @[youtube](ID_or_URL) — click-to-play YouTube embed
        {
          name: 'youtube',
          level: 'block',
          start: function(src) { return src.indexOf('@[youtube]'); },
          tokenizer: function(src) {
            var m = /^@\[youtube\]\(([^)]+)\)/.exec(src);
            if (m) {
              var id = extractYouTubeId(m[1].trim());
              if (id) return { type: 'youtube', raw: m[0], id: id };
            }
          },
          renderer: function(token) {
            var thumb = '/api/proxy/image?url=' + encodeURIComponent(
              'https://img.youtube.com/vi/' + token.id + '/maxresdefault.jpg');
            return '<div class="md-yt-wrap" data-yt-id="' + token.id + '">' +
              '<img src="' + thumb + '" class="md-yt-thumb" alt="YouTube video">' +
              '<button class="md-yt-play" type="button" aria-label="Play video">&#9654;</button>' +
              '</div>\n';
          }
        }
      ]
    });
  }

  // Click-to-play: swap thumbnail for iframe when the user clicks a YouTube embed.
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    var wrap = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.md-yt-wrap:not(.md-yt-active)');
    if (!wrap) return;
    var id = wrap.dataset.ytId;
    if (!id) return;
    wrap.classList.add('md-yt-active');
    var iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.src = 'https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/' + id + '?autoplay=1';
    iframe.setAttribute('frameborder', '0');
    iframe.setAttribute('allowfullscreen', '');
    iframe.setAttribute('allow', 'autoplay; accelerometer; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture');
    wrap.textContent = '';
    wrap.appendChild(iframe);
  });</script><script>(function() {
    if (window.location.pathname === '/banned') return;
    // Cookie already set and valid? Skip re-generation (saves a CDN round-trip
    // after the first visit).
    function getCookie(name) {
      var m = document.cookie.match('(?:^|; )' + name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&') + '=([^;]*)');
      return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
    }
    var existing = getCookie('ar-fp');
    if (existing && /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(existing)) {
      // Already have a fingerprint — just report it to the server so we get
      // the ban check and the user row update.
      fetch('/api/fingerprint', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ fingerprint: existing }),

          + '; max-age=31536000; path=/; SameSite=Strict';

        });

      .then(function(r) {

    var modal   = document.getElementById('ar-anime-modal');
    var q       = document.getElementById('ar-anime-modal-q');
    var results = document.getElementById('ar-anime-results');
    var btn     = document.getElementById('ar-anime-modal-btn');
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('ar-anime-modal-close');
    var _onSelect = null;
    var _searchTimer = null;
    var MSG_HINT     = "Type to search…";
    var MSG_SEARCHING= "Searching…";
    var MSG_EMPTY    = "No results found.";
    var MSG_ERROR    = "Search failed.";

    function _esc(s) {
      return String(s||'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
    }

    function _close() {
      if (!modal) return;
      modal.classList.add('closing');
      setTimeout(function() { modal.classList.remove('open','closing'); }, 180);
    }

    function _search() {
      var v = q ? q.value.trim() : '';
      if (v.length < 2) {
        if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_HINT)+'</div>';
        return;
      }
      if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_SEARCHING)+'</div>';
      fetch('/api/v1/anime/search?q='+encodeURIComponent(v)+'&limit=10')
        .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
        .then(function(items) {
          if (!results) return;
          if (!items || !items.length) {
            results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_EMPTY)+'</div>';
            return;
          }
          results.innerHTML = '';
          items.forEach(function(item) {
            var el = document.createElement('div');
            el.className = 'td-anime-result-item';
            el.innerHTML =
              '<img class="td-anime-result-thumb" src="'+_esc(item.thumbnail||'')+'" alt="" loading="lazy">'+
              '<div class="td-anime-result-info">'+
                '<div class="td-anime-result-title">'+_esc(item.title)+'</div>'+
                '<div class="td-anime-result-sub">'+_esc(item.anime_type)+' &bull; '+item.episodes+' ep &bull; '+_esc(item.status)+
                  (item.season_year ? ' &bull; '+_esc(item.season)+' '+item.season_year : '')+
                '</div>'+
              '</div>';
            el.addEventListener('click', function() {
              if (_onSelect) _onSelect(item);
              _onSelect = null;
              _close();
            });
            results.appendChild(el);
          });
        })
        .catch(function() {
          if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_ERROR)+'</div>';
        });
    }

    if (modal) {
      closeBtn && closeBtn.addEventListener('click', _close);
      modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === modal) _close(); });
    }
    if (q) {
      q.addEventListener('input', function() { clearTimeout(_searchTimer); _searchTimer = setTimeout(_search, 300); });
      q.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') { clearTimeout(_searchTimer); _search(); } });
    }
    if (btn) btn.addEventListener('click', function() { clearTimeout(_searchTimer); _search(); });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {

    });

  // ── Lift card into a fixed overlay so blurring main-content doesn't affect it ──
  var wrap = document.querySelector('.banned-wrap');
  if (wrap) {
    var overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.id = 'banned-overlay';
    overlay.appendChild(wrap);
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    wrap.style.visibility = '';
  }

  // ── Freeze the rest of the page ──
  document.body.classList.add('body-banned');
  ['main-nav', 'main-content', 'main-footer'].forEach(function(id) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.inert = true;
  });

  // ── Swap in network-ban messaging if ?reason=ip/fp ──
  var reason = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('reason');
  if (!reason) return;

  var L = {
    ip_title:    "Access Denied",
    ip_subtitle: "Your IP address or network has been blocked from accessing AniRena.",
    ip_body:     "If you believe this is an error (e.g. you are behind a shared or VPN IP), please contact support.",
    fp_title:    "Access Denied",
    fp_subtitle: "Potential ban evasion detected.",
    fp_body:     "Your browser has been flagged in connection with a previously suspended account. If you believe this is a mistake, please contact support.",
  };

  var title, subtitle, body;
  if (reason === 'ip') {
    title = L.ip_title; subtitle = L.ip_subtitle; body = L.ip_body;
  } else if (reason === 'fp') {
    title = L.fp_title; subtitle = L.fp_subtitle; body = L.fp_body;
  } else {
    return;
  }

  document.getElementById('banned-title').textContent       = title;
  document.getElementById('banned-subtitle').textContent    = subtitle;
  document.getElementById('banned-network-msg').textContent = body;
  document.getElementById('banned-body-acct').style.display    = 'none';
  document.getElementById('banned-body-network').style.display = '';
})();</script></main><footer id=(removed) role=contentinfo><div class=footer-inner><a class=footer-logo href=https://www.anirena.com hx-get=/ hx-push-url=https://www.anirena.com     // Strip raw HTML typed by users; alignment and YouTube are handled via extensions.
    // Override the image renderer: if the title is a plain number (e.g. "300" or "300px")
    // treat it as a pixel width rather than an alt-title.  max-width:100% CSS then
    // scales it down further when the container is narrower than the specified width.
    // Enable hard line-breaks so a single newline renders as <br>
    marked.use({ breaks: true });
    marked.use({
      renderer: {
        html: function() { return ''; },
        image: function(token) {
          var src   = token.href  || '';
          var alt   = (token.text  || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
          var title = token.title || '';
          var wm    = /^(\d+)(?:px)?$/.exec(title.trim());
          if (wm) {
            return '<img src="' + src + '" alt="' + alt + '" width="' + wm[1] + '">';
          }
          var ta = title ? ' title="' + title.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '"' : '';
          return '<img src="' + src + '" alt="' + alt + '"' + ta + '>';
        }
      }
    });

    // Helper: extract an 11-char YouTube video ID from a URL or bare ID.
    function extractYouTubeId(input) {
      var m = input.match(/(?:v=|youtu\.be\/|embed\/)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{11})/);
      if (m) return m[1];
      if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(input)) return input;
      return null;
    }

    marked.use({
      extensions: [
        // ::: left/center/right … ::: — alignment blocks
        {
          name: 'alignBlock',
          level: 'block',
          start: function(src) { return src.indexOf('\n:::'); },
          tokenizer: function(src) {
            var m = /^:::\s*(left|center|right)\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n?:::\s*(?:\r?\n|$)/i.exec(src);
            if (m) {
              var token = { type: 'alignBlock', raw: m[0], align: m[1].toLowerCase(), tokens: [] };
              this.lexer.blockTokens(m[2].trim(), token.tokens);
              return token;
            }
          },
          renderer: function(token) {
            return '<div align="' + token.align + '">' + this.parser.parse(token.tokens) + '</div>\n';
          }
        },
        // @[youtube](ID_or_URL) — click-to-play YouTube embed
        {
          name: 'youtube',
          level: 'block',
          start: function(src) { return src.indexOf('@[youtube]'); },
          tokenizer: function(src) {
            var m = /^@\[youtube\]\(([^)]+)\)/.exec(src);
            if (m) {
              var id = extractYouTubeId(m[1].trim());
              if (id) return { type: 'youtube', raw: m[0], id: id };
            }
          },
          renderer: function(token) {
            var thumb = '/api/proxy/image?url=' + encodeURIComponent(
              'https://img.youtube.com/vi/' + token.id + '/maxresdefault.jpg');
            return '<div class="md-yt-wrap" data-yt-id="' + token.id + '">' +
              '<img src="' + thumb + '" class="md-yt-thumb" alt="YouTube video">' +
              '<button class="md-yt-play" type="button" aria-label="Play video">&#9654;</button>' +
              '</div>\n';
          }
        }
      ]
    });
  }

  // Click-to-play: swap thumbnail for iframe when the user clicks a YouTube embed.
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    var wrap = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.md-yt-wrap:not(.md-yt-active)');
    if (!wrap) return;
    var id = wrap.dataset.ytId;
    if (!id) return;

    // Cookie already set and valid? Skip re-generation (saves a CDN round-trip
    // after the first visit).
    function getCookie(name) {
      var m = document.cookie.match('(?:^|; )' + name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&') + '=([^;]*)');
      return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;

    var results = document.getElementById('ar-anime-results');

    var MSG_HINT     = "Type to search…";
    var MSG_SEARCHING= "Searching…";
    var MSG_EMPTY    = "No results found.";
    var MSG_ERROR    = "Search failed.";

    function _esc(s) {
      return String(s||'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
    }

    function _close() {
      if (!modal) return;
      modal.classList.add('closing');
      setTimeout(function() { modal.classList.remove('open','closing'); }, 180);
    }

    function _search() {
      var v = q ? q.value.trim() : '';
      if (v.length < 2) {
        if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_HINT)+'</div>';
        return;
      }
      if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_SEARCHING)+'</div>';
      fetch('/api/v1/anime/search?q='+encodeURIComponent(v)+'&limit=10')
        .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
        .then(function(items) {
          if (!results) return;
          if (!items || !items.length) {
            results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_EMPTY)+'</div>';
            return;
          }
          results.innerHTML = '';
          items.forEach(function(item) {
            var el = document.createElement('div');

                '<div class="td-anime-result-sub">'+_esc(item.anime_type)+' &bull; '+item.episodes+' ep &bull; '+_esc(item.status)+
                  (item.season_year ? ' &bull; '+_esc(item.season)+' '+item.season_year : '')+
                '</div>'+
              '</div>';
            el.addEventListener('click', function() {
              if (_onSelect) _onSelect(item);
              _onSelect = null;
              _close();
            });
            results.appendChild(el);
          });
        })
        .catch(function() {
          if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_ERROR)+'</div>';
        });
    }

    if (modal) {
      closeBtn && closeBtn.addEventListener('click', _close);
      modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === modal) _close(); });
    }

      q.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') { clearTimeout(_searchTimer); _search(); } });
    }
    if (btn) btn.addEventListener('click', function() { clearTimeout(_searchTimer); _search(); });

    });

    // Strip raw HTML typed by users; alignment and YouTube are handled via extensions.
    // Override the image renderer: if the title is a plain number (e.g. "300" or "300px")
    // treat it as a pixel width rather than an alt-title.  max-width:100% CSS then
    // scales it down further when the container is narrower than the specified width.
    // Enable hard line-breaks so a single newline renders as <br>
    marked.use({ breaks: true });
    marked.use({
      renderer: {
        html: function() { return ''; },
        image: function(token) {
          var src   = token.href  || '';
          var alt   = (token.text  || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
          var title = token.title || '';
          var wm    = /^(\d+)(?:px)?$/.exec(title.trim());
          if (wm) {
            return '<img src="' + src + '" alt="' + alt + '" width="' + wm[1] + '">';
          }
          var ta = title ? ' title="' + title.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '"' : '';
          return '<img src="' + src + '" alt="' + alt + '"' + ta + '>';
        }
      }
    });

    // Helper: extract an 11-char YouTube video ID from a URL or bare ID.
    function extractYouTubeId(input) {
      var m = input.match(/(?:v=|youtu\.be\/|embed\/)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{11})/);
      if (m) return m[1];
      if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(input)) return input;
      return null;
    }

    marked.use({
      extensions: [
        // ::: left/center/right … ::: — alignment blocks
        {
          name: 'alignBlock',
          level: 'block',
          start: function(src) { return src.indexOf('\n:::'); },
          tokenizer: function(src) {
            var m = /^:::\s*(left|center|right)\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n?:::\s*(?:\r?\n|$)/i.exec(src);
            if (m) {
              var token = { type: 'alignBlock', raw: m[0], align: m[1].toLowerCase(), tokens: [] };
              this.lexer.blockTokens(m[2].trim(), token.tokens);
              return token;
            }
          },
          renderer: function(token) {
            return '<div align="' + token.align + '">' + this.parser.parse(token.tokens) + '</div>\n';
          }
        },
        // @[youtube](ID_or_URL) — click-to-play YouTube embed
        {
          name: 'youtube',
          level: 'block',
          start: function(src) { return src.indexOf('@[youtube]'); },
          tokenizer: function(src) {
            var m = /^@\[youtube\]\(([^)]+)\)/.exec(src);
            if (m) {
              var id = extractYouTubeId(m[1].trim());
              if (id) return { type: 'youtube', raw: m[0], id: id };
            }
          },
          renderer: function(token) {
            var thumb = '/api/proxy/image?url=' + encodeURIComponent(
              'https://img.youtube.com/vi/' + token.id + '/maxresdefault.jpg');
            return '<div class="md-yt-wrap" data-yt-id="' + token.id + '">' +
              '<img src="' + thumb + '" class="md-yt-thumb" alt="YouTube video">' +
              '<button class="md-yt-play" type="button" aria-label="Play video">&#9654;</button>' +
              '</div>\n';
          }
        }
      ]
    });
  }

  // Click-to-play: swap thumbnail for iframe when the user clicks a YouTube embed.
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    var wrap = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.md-yt-wrap:not(.md-yt-active)');
    if (!wrap) return;
    var id = wrap.dataset.ytId;
    if (!id) return;
    wrap.classList.add('md-yt-active');
    var iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.src = 'https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/' + id + '?autoplay=1';
    iframe.setAttribute('frameborder', '0');
    iframe.setAttribute('allowfullscreen', '');
    iframe.setAttribute('allow', 'autoplay; accelerometer; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture');
    wrap.textContent = '';
    wrap.appendChild(iframe);
  });</script><script>(function() {
    if (window.location.pathname === '/banned') return;
    // Cookie already set and valid? Skip re-generation (saves a CDN round-trip
    // after the first visit).
    function getCookie(name) {
      var m = document.cookie.match('(?:^|; )' + name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&') + '=([^;]*)');
      return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
    }
    var existing = getCookie('ar-fp');
    if (existing && /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(existing)) {
      // Already have a fingerprint — just report it to the server so we get
      // the ban check and the user row update.
      fetch('/api/fingerprint', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ fingerprint: existing }),
        credentials: 'same-origin',
      }).then(function(r) {
        if (r.status === 403) {
          window.location.href = '/banned?reason=fp';
        }
      }).catch(function() {});
      return;
    }
    import('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@fingerprintjs/fingerprintjs@4/dist/fp.esm.min.js')
      .then(function(FingerprintJS) { return FingerprintJS.load(); })

    var q       = document.getElementById('ar-anime-modal-q');
    var results = document.getElementById('ar-anime-results');
    var btn     = document.getElementById('ar-anime-modal-btn');

    var MSG_SEARCHING= "Searching…";
    var MSG_EMPTY    = "No results found.";
    var MSG_ERROR    = "Search failed.";

    function _esc(s) {
      return String(s||'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
    }

    function _close() {
      if (!modal) return;
      modal.classList.add('closing');
      setTimeout(function() { modal.classList.remove('open','closing'); }, 180);
    }

    function _search() {
      var v = q ? q.value.trim() : '';
      if (v.length < 2) {
        if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_HINT)+'</div>';
        return;
      }
      if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_SEARCHING)+'</div>';
      fetch('/api/v1/anime/search?q='+encodeURIComponent(v)+'&limit=10')
        .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
        .then(function(items) {
          if (!results) return;
          if (!items || !items.length) {
            results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_EMPTY)+'</div>';
            return;
          }
          results.innerHTML = '';
          items.forEach(function(item) {
            var el = document.createElement('div');
            el.className = 'td-anime-result-item';

              '<img class="td-anime-result-thumb" src="'+_esc(item.thumbnail||'')+'" alt="" loading="lazy">'+
              '<div class="td-anime-result-info">'+
                '<div class="td-anime-result-title">'+_esc(item.title)+'</div>'+
                '<div class="td-anime-result-sub">'+_esc(item.anime_type)+' &bull; '+item.episodes+' ep &bull; '+_esc(item.status)+
                  (item.season_year ? ' &bull; '+_esc(item.season)+' '+item.season_year : '')+
                '</div>'+
              '</div>';
            el.addEventListener('click', function() {
              if (_onSelect) _onSelect(item);
              _onSelect = null;
              _close();
            });
            results.appendChild(el);
          });
        })
        .catch(function() {
          if (results) results.innerHTML = '<div class="td-anime-results-hint">'+_esc(MSG_ERROR)+'</div>';
        });
    }

    if (modal) {
      closeBtn && closeBtn.addEventListener('click', _close);
      modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === modal) _close(); });
    }

      q.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') { clearTimeout(_searchTimer); _search(); } });
    }
    if (btn) btn.addEventListener('click', function() { clearTimeout(_searchTimer); _search(); });

    });
